1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any coding module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 742. The projective resolution has length at most 834 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by coding generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 742, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 834. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the coding object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 890. The moduli space has dimension at most 333 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on coding structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 890. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 333. QED.
